Snapshot captured 2026-09-28T23:46:00+00:00. Release target: 2026-09-30.

Restaurant tip: `215568faf361ee49807c95fe69e00fa71b6565f2`. Pharmacy-union tip: `89f39834e7688c7f93dc467b945c980ada708463`.

## Interpretation

The requested ledger date is 2026-09-28; the capture occurred after midnight in Asia/Karachi. Both September 28 and September 29 creation groups are explicit below. All 19 September 29 local creation timestamps shown here fall on September 28 UTC, matching the dated board batch. All matching local branches are retained so older or missing reflogs cannot silently hide a lane. No remote fetch was performed.

`git branch` committer dates describe the tip commit, not branch creation. Creation dates and base SHAs below come from retained `branch: Created from` reflog entries. Base means the SHA at branch creation, not a guessed current merge-base. Missing creation records are unknown, not proof the branch predates the release.

Five named lanes without ticket IDs were mapped by targeted Paperclip issue searches, with issue status captured at lookup. Other owning tickets are inferred from branch names, not verified assignment records; names without a ticket need board confirmation. Status is Git ancestry against the pinned restaurant/pharmacy tips, not a claim of ticket completion, review approval or test success. `ux/pos-redesign-spec` has multiple referencing tickets (WHA-1574, WHA-1583 and WHA-1601 among them); the board must select the composition owner rather than infer one from cross-references. Every candidate needs board selection and owner evidence before composition. Included tips should not be applied twice; tips outside both bases are not automatically eligible.

Reproduce the requested inventory:

```bash
git branch --list --format='%(refname:short) %(objectname:short) %(committerdate:short)' \
  'WHA-*' 'REST-*' 'PH-*' 'POS-UX-*' 'SA-*' 'board/*' 'ux/*'
git reflog show --format='%H|%gD|%gs' --date=iso-strict <branch>
git merge-base --is-ancestor <tip> <pinned-base>
```

Counts: **811 branches**; **0** created September 28 Asia/Karachi; **19** created September 29; **1** without retained creation evidence. `DOCS-staging-runbook` is outside the requested patterns and is listed separately below.
